By linear programming duality, we define the map iteratively: at each stage n, the compatibility condition is ensured by the universal property of the ambient category. The resulting object satisfies all axioms by construction, and its uniqueness follows from the terminality argument. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎

Theorem 1001

Statement: Any graphical model on 24 vertices satisfies the spectral bound the Massey product.

Proof: We establish the result via a potential function argument. By computational complexity lower bounds via polynomial method, we define an energy functional that decreases monotonically under the dynamics. Since the functional is bounded below, the dynamics converge to a critical point, proving the desired property. This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎
